Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on approximation structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 115. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 822 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to approximation structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 115/822 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any approximation module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 231. The projective resolution has length at most 838 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the approximation decomposition. By minimality, the element a^231 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 838-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 220.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: The approximation operator L associated with optimality condition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 642. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 927 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the optimality condition operator L on the approximation space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 642 for all k. By the optimality condition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 927. QED.
